// Our own names, with evidence in docs/discoveries.md. Sony and lsddecomp names live in their own files.

// Game .rodata: every symbol except jump tables stays out of rodata migration, so it is written
// with INCLUDE_RODATA at its source position (strings at parse time, jtbls with their function).
// Extra starts split labels where the owner changes.

// One bss record of 0x20 bytes, not 26 scalars:
// SsUtKeyOn and SsUtKeyOnV fill it byte by byte, SpuVmDoAllocate computes &EA28 - 2. All three
// turned out to be LIBSND VMANAGER (2026-10-15), so this is a LIBSND variable, not game data.
// Its neighbours: 0x8008EA0A is LIBGPU's (psyq/80020C74), 0x8008EA2C LIBSND's.
D_8008EA0C = 0x8008EA0C; // size:0x20

// Records that splat cut into fake labels from lwl/lwr and field offsets.
g_tileOffsets = 0x80087F30; // size:0xE0 // 6-byte x, y, z offsets in a tile copied into StagePlace +4 by Link_Find and the spawn pickers
g_spawnPlaceOffsets = 0x80088D3C; // size:0x1DC // 6-byte offset records copied by Sym_SpawnAtPlace into the placement query
g_sceneryParams = 0x8008E0A4; // size:0x24 // the SceneryParams the RandomizeParams functions and CreateKind fill, copied whole by Scenery_Reset
// Jump table without the zero word after it: that word is the .align 3 pad of jtbl_80011594.
jtbl_8001157C = 0x8001157C; // size:0x14
// 8-byte sdata pairs copied whole as structs, defined with their initializer in their C unit (splat.yaml .sdata).
g_viewDefaultScreenSize = 0x8008A8FC; // size:0x8 // ScreenPoint (0x100, 0xF0) copied to View +0x34 by View_Reset
g_bandPos = 0x8008AB68; // size:0x8 // (-100, -60), position of the first band, y + 30 for layout 2
g_bandSize = 0x8008AB70; // size:0x8 // (320, 144), size of the first band, each next band is 7 shorter

// Names with evidence (2026-10-15); the comment names the discoveries.md row.

DreamSys_ChooseStart = 0x8005A508; // type:func size:0x78 // slot 0x1C0, yesterday point, Spawn_PickFromMood, time limit, transition 0xB, predicted start stage seen in game (first dream of a new year) (see docs/discoveries.md#the-first-dream-of-a-new-year-starts-from-the-wrong-data)

DreamSys_ExecuteLink = 0x8005A928; // type:func size:0xA4 // sets and notifies the transition, then stage, timer reset in flashback mode, sound 0x90, DreamSys_ExecuteLink(12, 16) logged in hunt 8 (see docs/discoveries.md#the-start-place-sets-the-length-of-the-dream)

DreamSys_OnTileContact = 0x80058FAC; // type:func size:0x134 // slot 0xE0 message 4, touched tile into place, LinkStatic else LinkDynamic while dynamicLinkOn, RestorePosition, 14 put-backs then a link seen in hunt 8 (see docs/discoveries.md#where-a-random-link-sends-you)

g_fileLock = 0x8008A88C; // File_Lock sets 1, File_Unlock 0, File_OnVSync skips while set

g_fileRequestHead = 0x8008A894; // Head of the FileRequest list, File_NewRequest appends, File_FreeRequest unlinks, File_ServiceQueue runs it
g_fileAsyncMode = 0x8008A898; // 1 single seek or read (File_PollIo), 2 whole-file load (File_StepIo) chosen in File_OnVSync, cleared by File_EndOp
g_fileVSyncHooked = 0x8008A89C; // Set by File_HookVSync after VSyncCallback(File_OnVSync), cleared by File_UnhookVSync
